TL;DR: CT-Former：结合连续时间建模与因果Transformer的AKI早期预测模型，解决数据不规则性和模型可解释性问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型在处理不规则采样数据时存在困难，且序列架构的"黑盒"特性限制了临床信任度，需要开发既准确又透明的AKI预测工具

Method: 提出CT-Former框架，整合连续时间状态演化机制处理数据不规则性，采用因果注意力模块生成有向结构因果矩阵追踪生理冲击的历史起源，采用解耦的两阶段训练协议优化因果融合过程

Result: 在MIMIC-IV队列（N=18,419）上的广泛实验表明，CT-Former显著优于最先进的基线方法，证实了其透明架构在临床决策中的准确性和可信度

Conclusion: CT-Former通过建立历史异常与当前风险预测之间的清晰因果路径，提供了原生临床可解释性，为临床决策提供了准确且可信的工具

Abstract: Accurate early prediction of Acute Kidney Injury (AKI) is critical for timely clinical intervention. However, existing deep learning models struggle with irregularly sampled data and suffer from the opaque "black-box" nature of sequential architectures, strictly limiting clinical trust. To address these challenges, we propose CT-Former, integrating continuous-time modeling with a Causal-Transformer. To handle data irregularity without biased artificial imputation, our framework utilizes a continuous-time state evolution mechanism to naturally track patient temporal trajectories. To resolve the black-box problem, our Causal-Attention module abandons uninterpretable hidden state aggregation. Instead, it generates a directed structural causal matrix to identify and trace the exact historical onset of severe physiological shocks. By establishing clear causal pathways between historical anomalies and current risk predictions, CT-Former provides native clinical interpretability. Training follows a decoupled two-stage protocol to optimize the causal-fusion process independently. Extensive experiments on the MIMIC-IV cohort (N=18,419) demonstrate that CT-Former significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. The results confirm that our explicitly transparent architecture offers an accurate and trustworthy tool for clinical decision-making.

</details>


### [117] [Generative Augmentation of Imbalanced Flight Records for Flight Diversion Prediction: A Multi-objective Optimisation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20288)
*Karim Aly,Alexei Sharpanskykh,Jacco Hoekstra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出使用超参数优化的生成模型（TVAE、CTGAN、CopulaGAN）生成合成航班备降数据，以解决历史数据稀缺问题，并通过多目标优化框架提升预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航班备降是罕见但高影响事件，历史数据稀缺阻碍了机器学习模型的训练。需要解决数据稀缺问题以提高预测准确性和操作效率。

Method: 采用多目标优化框架结合自动超参数搜索，优化三种深度生成模型（TVAE、CTGAN、CopulaGAN），以高斯Copula模型作为统计基线。通过六阶段评估框架（真实性、多样性、操作有效性、统计相似性、保真度、预测效用）评估合成数据质量。

Result: 优化后的模型显著优于未优化版本，合成数据增强相比仅使用真实数据训练的模型大幅提高了备降预测准确性。

Conclusion: 超参数优化的生成模型能有效推进航空运输中罕见事件的预测建模，通过合成数据增强解决数据稀缺问题。

Abstract: Flight diversions are rare but high-impact events in aviation, making their reliable prediction vital for both safety and operational efficiency. However, their scarcity in historical records impedes the training of machine learning models utilised to predict them. This study addresses this scarcity gap by investigating how generative models can augment historical flight data with synthetic diversion records to enhance model training and improve predictive accuracy. We propose a multi-objective optimisation framework coupled with automated hyperparameter search to identify optimal configurations for three deep generative models: Tabular Variational Autoencoder (TVAE), Conditional Tabular Generative Adversarial Network (CTGAN), and CopulaGAN, with the Gaussian Copula (GC) model serving as a statistical baseline. The quality of the synthetic data was examined through a six-stage evaluation framework encompassing realism, diversity, operational validity, statistical similarity, fidelity, and predictive utility. Results show that the optimised models significantly outperform their non-optimised counterparts, and that synthetic augmentation substantially improves diversion prediction compared to models trained solely on real data. These findings demonstrate the effectiveness of hyperparameter-optimised generative models for advancing predictive modelling of rare events in air transportation.

</details>


### [118] [Synthetic Flight Data Generation Using Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20293)
*Karim Aly,Alexei Sharpanskykh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了使用生成模型（TVAE和GC）创建合成飞行数据的潜力，通过四阶段评估框架验证其质量，发现合成数据可用于训练与真实数据性能相当的航班延误预测模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空研究中数据稀缺和保密性问题日益突出，需要替代真实飞行记录的合成数据，同时增强历史数据集中罕见事件的样本，以训练预测关键事件（如航班延误、取消、改航和周转时间）的机器学习模型。

Method: 采用表格变分自编码器（TVAE）和高斯Copula（GC）两种生成模型来生成合成飞行信息，通过四阶段评估框架比较它们在统计相似性、保真度、多样性和预测效用方面的表现。

Result: GC在统计相似性和保真度方面表现更好，但计算成本高，不适用于大型数据集；TVAE能高效处理大型数据集，支持可扩展的合成数据生成。合成数据训练的航班延误预测模型准确性与真实数据训练的模型相当。

Conclusion: 合成飞行数据可作为真实数据的有效替代，支持航空运输中的预测建模，TVAE因其可扩展性更适合大规模应用，为利用合成数据增强航空预测模型开辟了道路。

Abstract: The increasing adoption of synthetic data in aviation research offers a promising solution to data scarcity and confidentiality challenges. This study investigates the potential of generative models to produce realistic synthetic flight data and evaluates their quality through a comprehensive four-stage assessment framework. The need for synthetic flight data arises from their potential to serve as an alternative to confidential real-world records and to augment rare events in historical datasets. These enhanced datasets can then be used to train machine learning models that predict critical events, such as flight delays, cancellations, diversions, and turnaround times. Two generative models, Tabular Variational Autoencoder (TVAE) and Gaussian Copula (GC), are adapted to generate synthetic flight information and compared based on their ability to preserve statistical similarity, fidelity, diversity, and predictive utility. Results indicate that while GC achieves higher statistical similarity and fidelity, its computational cost hinders its applicability to large datasets. In contrast, TVAE efficiently handles large datasets and enables scalable synthetic data generation. The findings demonstrate that synthetic data can support flight delay prediction models with accuracy comparable to those trained on real data. These results pave the way for leveraging synthetic flight data to enhance predictive modeling in air transportation.

</details>


### [119] [Formalising the Logit Shift Induced by LoRA: A Technical Note](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20313)
*Xiang Shi,Shuaizhi Cheng,Mingwei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该技术笔记首次对LoRA引起的logit偏移和事实边界变化进行一阶形式化，使用围绕基础模型轨迹的Fréchet近似，将多层LoRA效应分解为层间贡献的线性求和与表示层间耦合的高阶余项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) 对模型输出的具体影响机制，特别是logit偏移和事实边界变化，为LoRA的理论分析提供形式化框架。

Method: 使用围绕基础模型轨迹的一阶Fréchet近似，将多层LoRA效应分解为层间贡献的线性求和与表示层间耦合的高阶余项。

Result: 建立了LoRA效应的数学形式化框架，展示了多层LoRA效应可分解为线性层间贡献和高阶耦合项，为理解LoRA工作机制提供了理论分析工具。

Conclusion: 该工作为LoRA的理论分析提供了首个形式化框架，揭示了LoRA效应的可分解性，为后续的优化设计和性能分析奠定了基础。

Abstract: This technical note provides a first-order formalisation of the logit shift and fact-margin change induced by Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA). Using a first-order Fréchet approximation around the base model trajectory, we show that the multi-layer LoRA effect can be decomposed into a linear summation of layerwise contributions and a higher-order remainder term representing inter-layer coupling.

</details>


### [120] [Cold-Start Forecasting of New Product Life-Cycles via Conditional Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20370)
*Ruihan Zhou,Zishi Zhang,Jinhui Han,Yijie Peng,Xiaowei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CDLF是一个条件生成框架，用于在冷启动条件下预测新产品生命周期轨迹，结合静态描述符、相似产品参考轨迹和新观测数据，提供灵活的多模态预测分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新产品生命周期预测在上市前和上市初期面临冷启动问题，此时产品特定历史数据有限或缺失，而企业需要在需求模式可观测前做出决策，早期信号稀疏、嘈杂且不稳定。

Method: 提出条件扩散生命周期预测器(CDLF)，结合三种信息源：静态描述符（产品结构化预上市特征）、相似产品参考轨迹、以及新到观测数据（如可用）。该框架允许基于相关产品上下文条件化预测，无需重新训练即可随时间自适应更新，在极端数据稀缺下生成灵活的多模态预测分布。

Result: 在英特尔微处理器SKU生命周期和开源大语言模型库平台采用研究中，CDLF比经典扩散模型、贝叶斯更新方法和其他最先进的机器学习基线提供更准确的点预测和更高质量的概率预测。方法满足递归生成的水平均匀分布误差界限一致性。

Conclusion: CDLF有效解决了新产品生命周期预测中的冷启动问题，通过整合多源信息提供可靠预测，支持上市规划、资源分配和早期风险评估，在数据稀缺条件下表现优于现有方法。

Abstract: Forecasting the life-cycle trajectory of a newly launched product is important for launch planning, resource allocation, and early risk assessment. This task is especially difficult in the pre-launch and early post-launch phases, when product-specific outcome history is limited or unavailable, creating a cold-start problem. In these phases, firms must make decisions before demand patterns become reliably observable, while early signals are often sparse, noisy, and unstable We propose the Conditional Diffusion Life-cycle Forecaster (CDLF), a conditional generative framework for forecasting new-product life-cycle trajectories under cold start. CDLF combines three sources of information: static descriptors, reference trajectories from similar products, and newly arriving observations when available. Here, static descriptors refer to structured pre-launch characteristics of the product, such as category, price tier, brand or organization identity, scale, and access conditions. This structure allows the model to condition forecasts on relevant product context and to update them adaptively over time without retraining, yielding flexible multi-modal predictive distributions under extreme data scarcity. The method satisfies consistency with a horizon-uniform distributional error bound for recursive generation. Across studies on Intel microprocessor stock keeping unit (SKU) life cycles and the platform-mediated adoption of open large language model repositories, CDLF delivers more accurate point forecasts and higher-quality probabilistic forecasts than classical diffusion models, Bayesian updating approaches, and other state-of-the-art machine-learning baselines.

</details>


### [121] [Towards Event-Aware Forecasting in DeFi: Insights from On-chain Automated Market Maker Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20374)
*Huaiyu Jia,Jiehshun You,Yizhi Luo,Jingyu Liu,Shuo Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究构建了包含890万条链上事件记录的数据集，并提出不确定性加权均方误差损失函数，将区块间隔回归项纳入时间点过程目标函数，显著降低了时间预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动做市商作为DeFi核心基础设施，其价格动态主要由链上事件触发而非连续响应链下信息，但现有研究缺乏对AMM微观结构动态的分析，缺少多协议细粒度事件分类数据集和有效的事件感知建模框架。

Method: 1) 构建包含Pendle、Uniswap v3、Aave和Morpho四个代表性AMM协议的890万条链上事件记录数据集，包含精确的交易类型和区块高度时间戳标注；2) 提出不确定性加权均方误差损失函数，通过同方差性加权不确定性，将区块间隔回归项纳入传统时间点过程目标函数。

Result: 在八个先进的时间点过程架构上进行广泛实验，该损失函数将时间预测误差平均降低56.41%，同时保持事件类型预测的准确性，为AMM生态系统中的事件感知预测建立了稳健基准。

Conclusion: 该工作为建模链上价格发现的离散性和事件驱动特性提供了必要的数据基础和方法框架，所有数据集和源代码均已公开。

Abstract: Automated Market Makers (AMMs), as a core infrastructure of decentralized finance (DeFi), uniquely drive on-chain asset pricing through a deterministic reserve ratio mechanism. Unlike traditional markets, AMM price dynamics is triggered largely by on-chain events (e.g., swap) that change the reserve ratio, rather than by continuous responses to off-chain information. This makes event-level analysis crucial for understanding price formation mechanisms in AMMs. However, existing research generally neglects the micro-structural dynamics at the AMMs level, lacking both a comprehensive dataset covering multiple protocols with fine-grained event classification and an effective framework for event-aware modeling. To fill this gap, we construct a dataset containing 8.9 million on-chain event records from four representative AMMs protocols: Pendle, Uniswap v3, Aave and Morpho, with precise annotations of transaction type and block height timestamps. Furthermore, we propose an Uncertainty Weighted Mean Squared Error (UWM) loss function, which incorporates the block interval regression term into the traditional Time-Point Process (TPP) objective function by weighting the uncertainty with homoscedasticity. Extensive experiments on eight advanced TPP architectures demonstrate that this loss function reduces the time prediction error by an average of 56.41\% while maintaining the accuracy of event type prediction, establishing a robust benchmark for event-aware prediction in the AMMs ecosystem. This work provides the necessary data foundation and methodological framework for modeling the discreteness and event-driven characteristics of on-chain price discovery. All datasets and source code are publicly available. https://github.com/yosen-king/Deep-AMM-Events

</details>


### [122] [Calibrating conditional risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20409)
*Andrey Vasilyev,Yikai Wang,Xiaocheng Li,Guanting Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出并研究了条件风险校准问题，即估计预测模型在给定输入特征条件下的期望损失，建立了该问题与标准回归任务的等价性，并在分类和回归场景中进行了理论分析和实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性量化方法主要关注概率校准，但缺乏对条件风险（即给定输入特征下的期望损失）的系统研究。条件风险校准对于需要精确评估模型在不同输入条件下性能的应用（如学习延迟框架中的决策制定）至关重要。

Method: 将条件风险校准问题形式化为回归任务，证明其与标准回归的等价性。在分类场景中，建立条件风险校准与个体/条件概率校准的理论联系。通过系统实验验证理论发现，并在学习延迟框架中展示实际应用。

Result: 理论分析表明条件风险校准本质上是一个回归问题。实验验证了理论发现，展示了条件风险校准在学习延迟框架中的实际价值，为不确定性感知决策提供了定性和定量评估指导。

Conclusion: 条件风险校准是一个独立且重要的机器学习问题，与现有不确定性量化方法相关但不同。该研究为不确定性感知决策提供了理论基础和实践指导，特别是在需要精确评估模型条件性能的应用场景中。

Abstract: We introduce and study the problem of calibrating conditional risk, which involves estimating the expected loss of a prediction model conditional on input features. We analyze this problem in both classification and regression settings and show that it is fundamentally equivalent to a standard regression task. For classification settings, we further establish a connection between conditional risk calibration and individual/conditional probability calibration, and develop theoretical insights for the performance metric. This reveals that while conditional risk calibration is related to existing uncertainty quantification problems, it remains a distinct and standalone machine learning problem. Empirically, we validate our theoretical findings and demonstrate the practical implications of conditional risk calibration in the learning to defer (L2D) framework. Our systematic experiments provide both qualitative and quantitative assessments, offering guidance for future research in uncertainty-aware decision-making.

</details>


### [123] [Scalable AI Inference: Performance Analysis and Optimization of AI Model Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20420)
*Hung Cuong Pham,Fatih Gedikli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估并优化了基于BentoML的AI推理系统性能，通过三种真实工作负载场景测试，使用RoBERTa情感分析模型，分析延迟和吞吐量指标，并实施多级优化策略提升系统效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI研究通常侧重于模型设计和算法性能，而部署和推理在实际应用中至关重要却相对未被充分探索。本研究旨在填补这一空白，通过与graphworks.ai合作开发的BentoML推理系统，研究可扩展模型服务的性能优化。

Method: 首先在三种真实工作负载场景下建立基线性能：稳态、突发和高强度工作负载，使用预训练的RoBERTa情感分析模型，流量模式遵循伽马和指数分布。收集延迟百分位数和吞吐量等关键指标，识别推理管道瓶颈。然后引入运行时、服务和部署级别的多级优化策略，并在相同工作负载条件下重新评估优化后系统，通过统计分析量化改进效果。

Result: 研究展示了实现高效可扩展AI推理的实用策略：分析了延迟和吞吐量在不同工作负载下的扩展情况，量化了运行时、服务和部署级别优化对响应时间的影响，并评估了单节点K3s集群部署在中断期间的弹性表现。优化后的系统在性能指标上相比基线有显著提升。

Conclusion: 该研究为基于BentoML的AI推理系统提供了实用的性能优化策略，证明了多级优化方法在提升系统效率和可扩展性方面的有效性，为实际部署中的性能调优提供了实证依据。

Abstract: AI research often emphasizes model design and algorithmic performance, while deployment and inference remain comparatively underexplored despite being critical for real-world use. This study addresses that gap by investigating the performance and optimization of a BentoML-based AI inference system for scalable model serving developed in collaboration with graphworks.ai. The evaluation first establishes baseline performance under three realistic workload scenarios. To ensure a fair and reproducible assessment, a pre-trained RoBERTa sentiment analysis model is used throughout the experiments. The system is subjected to traffic patterns following gamma and exponential distributions in order to emulate real-world usage conditions, including steady, bursty, and high-intensity workloads. Key performance metrics, such as latency percentiles and throughput, are collected and analyzed to identify bottlenecks in the inference pipeline. Based on the baseline results, optimization strategies are introduced at multiple levels of the serving stack to improve efficiency and scalability. The optimized system is then reevaluated under the same workload conditions, and the results are compared with the baseline using statistical analysis to quantify the impact of the applied improvements. The findings demonstrate practical strategies for achieving efficient and scalable AI inference with BentoML. The study examines how latency and throughput scale under varying workloads, how optimizations at the runtime, service, and deployment levels affect response time, and how deployment in a single-node K3s cluster influences resilience during disruptions.

</details>


### [124] [Unlocking the Forecasting Economy: A Suite of Datasets for the Full Lifecycle of Prediction Market: [Experiments \& Analysis]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20421)
*Huaiyu Jia,Luofeng Zhou,Wentao Zhang,Lin William Cong,Siguang Li,Shuo Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首个去中心化预测市场全生命周期数据集，整合Polymarket的市场创建、交易、预言机交互等异构数据，提供770k市场记录、943M交易记录和2M预言机事件


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化预测市场（如Polymarket）的数据分散在链上和链下异构源中，缺乏统一、持续维护的全生命周期数据集，限制了市场分析和研究应用

Method: 构建统一关系型数据系统，通过标识符解析、链上恢复和增量更新，整合市场元数据、逐笔交易记录和预言机解决事件三个规范层

Result: 创建了2020年10月至2026年3月的完整数据集，包含超过77万市场记录、9.43亿交易记录和近200万预言机事件，数据可重现且可扩展

Conclusion: 该数据集为预测市场研究提供了首个全生命周期数据基础设施，通过NBA结果校准和CPI预期重建等案例验证了其分析价值和应用潜力

Abstract: Prediction markets are markets for trading claims on future events, such as presidential elections, and their prices provide continuously updated signals of collective beliefs. In decentralized platforms such as Polymarket, the market lifecycle spans market creation, token registration, trading, oracle interaction, dispute, and final settlement, yet the corresponding data are fragmented across heterogeneous off-chain and on-chain sources. We present the first continuously maintained dataset suite for the full lifecycle of decentralized prediction markets, built on Polymarket. To address the challenges of large-scale cross-source integration, incomplete linkage, and continuous synchronization, we build a unified relational data system that integrates three canonical layers: market metadata, fill-level trading records, and oracle-resolution events, through identifier resolution, on-chain recovery, and incremental updates. The resulting dataset spans October 2020 to March 2026 and comprises more than 770 thousand market records, over 943 million fill records, and nearly 2 million oracle events. We describe the data model, collection pipeline, and consistency mechanisms that make the dataset reproducible and extensible, and we demonstrate its utility through descriptive analyses of market activity and two downstream case studies: NBA outcome calibration and CPI expectation reconstruction.

</details>


### [125] [Efficient Test-Time Inference via Deterministic Exploration of Truncated Decoding Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20500)
*Xueyan Li,Johannes Zenn,Ekaterina Fadeeva,Guinan Su,Mrinmaya Sachan,Jonas Geiping*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出确定性解码方法DLE，通过系统枚举不同的叶子节点而非重复采样，提高推理效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自一致性方法在数学、代码等约束领域中计算效率低下，因为它采用有放回采样，会重复访问相同的高概率前缀和重复补全

Method: 提出Distinct Leaf Enumeration (DLE)，将截断采样视为剪枝解码树的遍历，系统枚举不同的叶子节点而非有放回采样

Result: DLE比随机自一致性探索更高质量的推理轨迹，在数学、编码和一般推理任务上表现更好

Conclusion: DLE通过算法上增加截断搜索空间的覆盖率和系统上重用共享前缀、减少冗余标记生成，提高了推理效率

Abstract: Self-consistency boosts inference-time performance by sampling multiple reasoning traces in parallel and voting. However, in constrained domains like math and code, this strategy is compute-inefficient because it samples with replacement, repeatedly revisiting the same high-probability prefixes and duplicate completions. We propose Distinct Leaf Enumeration (DLE), a deterministic decoding method that treats truncated sampling as traversal of a pruned decoding tree and systematically enumerates distinct leaves instead of sampling with replacement. This strategy improves inference efficiency in two ways. Algorithmically, it increases coverage of the truncated search space under a fixed budget by exploring previously unvisited high-probability branches. Systemically, it reuses shared prefixes and reduces redundant token generation. Empirically, DLE explores higher-quality reasoning traces than stochastic self-consistency, yielding better performance on math, coding, and general reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [126] [Amortized Vine Copulas for High-Dimensional Density and Information Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20568)
*Houman Safaai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VDC是一种摊销的藤式Copula管道，通过训练单个二元去噪模型并在所有藤边重复使用，结合IPFP/Sinkhorn投影保持Copula解释性，实现高效高维依赖建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统藤式Copula管道可解释但计算昂贵，神经估计器灵活但缺乏结构。需要一种既能保持藤式Copula的精确似然和解释性，又能实现高效计算的高维依赖建模方法。

Method: 提出Vine Denoising Copula (VDC)：训练单个二元去噪模型，给定伪观测值预测密度网格；应用IPFP/Sinkhorn投影确保非负性、单位质量和均匀边际；在GPU上执行推理替代重复的逐边优化。

Result: 在合成和真实数据基准测试中，VDC实现了强大的二元密度准确性、有竞争力的互信息/总相关性估计，以及高维藤拟合的显著加速。使显式信息估计和依赖分解在原本计算昂贵的规模下变得可行。

Conclusion: VDC结合了藤式Copula的精确似然和解释性与神经方法的计算效率，为高维依赖建模提供了实用解决方案，尽管条件下游推理仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Modeling high-dimensional dependencies while keeping likelihoods tractable remains challenging. Classical vine-copula pipelines are interpretable but can be expensive, while many neural estimators are flexible but less structured. In this work, we propose Vine Denoising Copula (VDC), an amortized vine-copula pipeline that trains a single bivariate denoising model and reuses it across all vine edges. For each edge, given pseudo-observations, the model predicts a density grid. We then apply an IPFP/Sinkhorn projection that enforces non-negativity, unit mass, and uniform marginals. This keeps the exact vine likelihood and preserves the usual copula interpretation while replacing repeated per-edge optimization with GPU inference. Across synthetic and real-data benchmarks, VDC delivers strong bivariate density accuracy, competitive MI/TC estimation, and substantial speedups for high-dimensional vine fitting. In practice, these gains make explicit information estimation and dependence decomposition feasible at scales where repeated vine fitting would otherwise be costly, although conditional downstream inference remains mixed.

</details>


### [127] [A Hierarchical MARL-Based Approach for Coordinated Retail P2P Trading and Wholesale Market Participation of DERs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20586)
*Patrick Wilk,Ethan Cantor,Yikui Liu,Jie Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于分层多智能体深度强化学习的市场参与框架，使产消者能够参与P2P零售拍卖，并聚合这些智能产消者以促进DER在批发市场的有效参与，最终通过Stackelberg博弈协调该框架以提升市场性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力能源部门向去中心化转型，终端用电部门电气化程度提高，分布式能源资源（DERs）广泛采用，需要DERs积极参与电力市场以支持电网运行。双向能源和通信流成为标准，智能、易部署、资源节约的需求侧参与对确保电网运行灵活性和市场效率至关重要。

Method: 提出基于分层多智能体深度强化学习（MARL）的市场参与框架：1）个体产消者参与点对点零售拍卖；2）聚合智能产消者促进DER在批发市场的有效参与；3）使用Stackelberg博弈协调分层MARL框架。

Result: 该框架能够实现：1）个体产消者通过MARL智能参与P2P零售市场；2）DERs通过聚合有效参与批发市场；3）通过Stackelberg博弈协调提升整体市场性能。

Conclusion: 提出的分层MARL框架结合Stackelberg博弈协调，能够有效促进DERs在零售和批发市场的智能参与，支持电网去中心化转型，提高市场效率和运行灵活性。

Abstract: The ongoing shift towards decentralization of the electric energy sector, driven by the growing electrification across end-use sectors, and widespread adoption of distributed energy resources (DERs), necessitates their active participation in the electricity markets to support grid operations. Furthermore, with bi-directional energy and communication flows becoming standard, intelligent, easy-to-deploy, resource-conservative demand-side participation is expected to play a critical role in securing power grid operational flexibility and market efficiency. This work proposes a market engagement framework that leverages a hierarchical multi-agent deep reinforcement learning (MARL) approach to enable individual prosumers to participate in peer-to-peer retail auctions and further aggregate these intelligent prosumers to facilitate effective DER participation in wholesale markets. Ultimately, a Stackelberg game is proposed to coordinate this hierarchical MARL-based DER market participation framework toward enhanced market performance.

</details>


### [128] [Differentially Private Clustered Federated Learning with Privacy-Preserving Initialization and Normality-Driven Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20596)
*Jie Xu,Haaris Mehmood,Rogier Van Dalen,Karthikeyan Saravanan,Mete Ozay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PINA：一种两阶段隐私保护聚类联邦学习框架，通过LoRA适配器微调和压缩草图共享解决DP噪声下CFL的挑战，在隐私预算ε∈{2,8}时平均准确率提升2.9%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习在异构数据下收敛慢、泛化差，聚类联邦学习(CFL)通过用户分簇降低簇内异构性，但结合差分隐私(DP)时面临挑战：DP噪声使客户端更新过度噪声化，服务器无法用低噪声聚合更新初始化簇中心

Method: 提出PINA两阶段框架：1) 客户端微调轻量级LoRA适配器并私有共享压缩更新草图；2) 服务器利用草图构建鲁棒簇中心，引入正态性驱动的聚合机制提升收敛性和鲁棒性

Result: 在隐私预算ε∈{2,8}时，PINA平均准确率比最先进的DP-FL算法提升2.9%，在保持聚类FL优势的同时提供对不可信服务器的正式隐私保证

Conclusion: PINA成功解决了DP噪声下CFL的挑战，通过压缩草图共享和正态性驱动聚合机制，在保证正式隐私的同时提升了模型性能，为异构数据下的隐私保护联邦学习提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables training of a global model while keeping raw data on end-devices. Despite this, FL has shown to leak private user information and thus in practice, it is often coupled with methods such as differential privacy (DP) and secure vector sum to provide formal privacy guarantees to its participants. In realistic cross-device deployments, the data are highly heterogeneous, so vanilla federated learning converges slowly and generalizes poorly. Clustered federated learning (CFL) mitigates this by segregating users into clusters, leading to lower intra-cluster data heterogeneity. Nevertheless, coupling CFL with DP remains challenging: the injected DP noise makes individual client updates excessively noisy, and the server is unable to initialize cluster centroids with the less noisy aggregated updates. To address this challenge, we propose PINA, a two-stage framework that first lets each client fine-tune a lightweight low-rank adaptation (LoRA) adapter and privately share a compressed sketch of the update. The server leverages these sketches to construct robust cluster centroids. In the second stage, PINA introduces a normality-driven aggregation mechanism that improves convergence and robustness. Our method retains the benefits of clustered FL while providing formal privacy guarantees against an untrusted server. Extensive evaluations show that our proposed method outperforms state-of-the-art DP-FL algorithms by an average of 2.9% in accuracy for privacy budgets (epsilon in {2, 8}).

</details>


### [129] [Occupancy Reward Shaping: Improving Credit Assignment for Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20627)
*Aravind Venugopal,Jiayu Chen,Xudong Wu,Chongyi Zheng,Benjamin Eysenbach,Jeff Schneider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Occupancy Reward Shaping方法，利用世界模型中的时间信息进行信用分配，解决稀疏奖励下的长期目标导向学习问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作与长期后果之间的时间延迟使得从数据中学习目标导向行为时的信用分配具有挑战性。生成世界模型捕获了未来状态的分布，表明它们包含了时间信息，但如何提取这些时间信息进行信用分配是核心问题。

Method: 形式化世界模型中时间信息如何编码世界底层几何结构。利用最优传输，从学习到的占用测度模型中提取几何信息，转化为捕获目标到达信息的奖励函数。该方法称为Occupancy Reward Shaping。

Result: ORS在理论上不改变最优策略，在13个多样化的长时程运动和控制任务上实证性能提升2.2倍。在真实世界的3个托卡马克核聚变控制任务中也展示了有效性。

Conclusion: Occupancy Reward Shaping方法通过提取世界模型中的几何信息进行奖励塑形，有效缓解稀疏奖励设置中的信用分配问题，在模拟和真实世界任务中均表现出显著性能提升。

Abstract: The temporal lag between actions and their long-term consequences makes credit assignment a challenge when learning goal-directed behaviors from data. Generative world models capture the distribution of future states an agent may visit, indicating that they have captured temporal information. How can that temporal information be extracted to perform credit assignment? In this paper, we formalize how the temporal information stored in world models encodes the underlying geometry of the world. Leveraging optimal transport, we extract this geometry from a learned model of the occupancy measure into a reward function that captures goal-reaching information. Our resulting method, Occupancy Reward Shaping, largely mitigates the problem of credit assignment in sparse reward settings. ORS provably does not alter the optimal policy, yet empirically improves performance by 2.2x across 13 diverse long-horizon locomotion and manipulation tasks. Moreover, we demonstrate the effectiveness of ORS in the real world for controlling nuclear fusion on 3 Tokamak control tasks.
  Code: https://github.com/aravindvenu7/occupancy_reward_shaping; Website: https://aravindvenu7.github.io/website/ors/

</details>


### [130] [GRPO-VPS: Enhancing Group Relative Policy Optimization with Verifiable Process Supervision for Effective Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20659)
*Jingyi Wang,Lei Zhu,Tengjin Weng,Song-Li Wu,Haochen Tan,Jierun Chen,Chaofan Tao,Haoli Bai,Lu Hou,Lifeng Shang,Xiao-Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种通过探测模型在推理轨迹中对正确答案的信念来进行模型无关、可验证的过程监督方法，改进了GRPO中的信用分配问题，在数学和通用领域基准上取得了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）通过直接结果验证提升了LLMs的推理能力。GRPO方法虽然消除了批评者模型的需求，但在中间步骤的信用分配上存在问题：它无法区分有效的推理策略，导致过度思考。需要一种更精细的过程监督方法来改进轨迹级反馈。

Method: 提出模型无关、可验证的过程监督方法：1）将生成过程分割为离散步骤；2）在每个分割边界处追踪模型对附加正确答案的条件概率；3）计算可解释的段级进展度量；4）利用这些度量细化GRPO的轨迹级反馈。该方法避免了昂贵的蒙特卡洛模拟或辅助模型产生的中间监督。

Result: 在数学和通用领域基准测试中，相比GRPO方法取得了一致性改进：数学任务上准确率提升最高达2.6个百分点，推理长度减少13.7%；通用领域任务上准确率提升最高达2.4个百分点，推理长度减少4%。展示了强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过探测模型在推理轨迹中对正确答案的信念来进行过程监督，能够实现更精准、样本效率更高的策略更新，有效解决了GRPO中的信用分配问题，在多个基准和模型上验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has advanced the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) by leveraging direct outcome verification instead of learned reward models. Building on this paradigm, Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) eliminates the need for critic models but suffers from indiscriminate credit assignment for intermediate steps, which limits its ability to identify effective reasoning strategies and incurs overthinking. In this work, we introduce a model-free and verifiable process supervision via probing the model's belief in the correct answer throughout its reasoning trajectory. By segmenting the generation into discrete steps and tracking the conditional probability of the correct answer appended at each segment boundary, we efficiently compute interpretable segment-wise progress measurements to refine GRPO's trajectory-level feedback. This approach enables more targeted and sample-efficient policy updates, while avoiding the need for intermediate supervision derived from costly Monte Carlo rollouts or auxiliary models. Experiments on mathematical and general-domain benchmarks show consistent gains over GRPO across diverse models: up to 2.6-point accuracy improvements and 13.7% reasoning-length reductions on math tasks, and up to 2.4 points and 4% on general-domain tasks, demonstrating strong generalization.

</details>


### [131] [Improving clinical interpretability of linear neuroimaging models through feature whitening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20675)
*Sara Petiton,Antoine Grigis,Raphaël Vock,Edouard Duchesnay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于神经解剖学先验知识的白化方法，用于解耦相关脑区特征，提高线性模型权重的可解释性，同时保持预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性模型在计算神经影像学中广泛用于识别与脑病理相关的生物标志物，但学习到的权重难以解释，缺乏临床意义。这主要是因为脑区之间存在固有相关性，导致线性权重反映的是共享贡献而非区域特异性贡献。特别是左右半球同源结构等区域组存在强解剖相关性。

Method: 提出一种基于神经解剖学先验知识的白化方法：1）对已知共享方差的区域组应用白化处理，解耦相关脑测量中的重叠信息；2）提出正则化变体，允许控制解相关程度；3）与PCA或ICA不同，该方法在保留完整输入信号的同时解相关解剖学信息区域对，专门用于特征解释而非特征选择。

Result: 在双相情感障碍和精神分裂症与健康对照的分类任务中，使用感兴趣区域特征进行评估。结果表明：白化处理提高了模型权重的可解释性，同时保持了预测性能。

Conclusion: 该方法为将线性模型输出与神经生物学机制联系起来提供了一个稳健框架。通过解耦解剖相关脑区的共享方差，白化处理显著改善了线性模型权重的可解释性，使其更易于临床解释和神经生物学机制分析。

Abstract: Linear models are widely used in computational neuroimaging to identify biomarkers associated with brain pathologies. However, interpreting the learned weights remains challenging, as they do not always yield clinically meaningful insights. This difficulty arises in part from the inherent correlation between brain regions, which causes linear weights to reflect shared rather than region-specific contributions. In particular, some groups of regions, including homologous structures in the left and right hemispheres, are known to exhibit strong anatomical correlations. In this work, we leverage this prior neuroanatomical knowledge to introduce a whitening approach applied to groups of regions with known shared variance, designed to disentangle overlapping information across correlated brain measures. We additionally propose a regularized variant that allows controlled tuning of the degree of decorrelation. We evaluate this method using region-of-interest features in two psychiatric classification tasks, distinguishing individuals with bipolar disorder or schizophrenia from healthy controls. Importantly, unlike PCA or ICA which use whitening as a dimensionality reduction step, our approach decorrelates anatomically informed pairs of neuroanatomical regions while retaining the full input signal, making it specifically suited for feature interpretation rather than feature selection. Our findings demonstrate that whitening improves the interpretability of model weights while preserving predictive performance, providing a robust framework for linking linear model outputs to neurobiological mechanisms.

</details>


### [132] [Variance Is Not Importance: Structural Analysis of Transformer Compressibility Across Model Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20682)
*Samuel Salfati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对GPT-2和Mistral 7B进行40+实验的系统性Transformer压缩研究，揭示了五个结构特性及其对压缩的启示


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统研究Transformer模型压缩的可行性和限制，探索各种压缩技术（谱压缩、块级函数替换、旋转量化等）的实际效果，为高效模型部署提供理论指导

Method: 在GPT-2（124M参数）和Mistral 7B（7.24B参数）上进行40多个实验，分析谱压缩、块级函数替换、旋转量化、激活几何和自适应早退等技术，通过CCA、R²等指标量化评估

Result: 发现五个关键结构特性：1）方差≠重要性；2）块线性是条件性的；3）重构墙现象；4）线性随深度增加；5）约30%token计算简单。单块线性替换实现34倍压缩但困惑度增加1.71，多块替换因误差累积失败

Conclusion: 静态后训练压缩存在根本性限制，自适应、按token计算是更有效的方向，为高效Transformer部署提供了新的理论框架和实践指导

Abstract: We present a systematic empirical study of transformer compression through over 40 experiments on GPT-2 (124M parameters) and Mistral 7B (7.24B parameters). Our analysis covers spectral compression, block-level function replacement, rotation-based quantization, activation geometry, and adaptive early exit.
  We identify five structural properties relevant to compression. (1) Variance is not importance: high-variance activation directions are approximately 96 percent uncorrelated with predictive directions (measured via CCA), and projecting onto these subspaces preserves over 90 percent of variance while degrading perplexity. (2) Block linearity is conditional: transformer blocks are approximately linear (R^2 ~ 0.95 on GPT-2, 0.93 on Mistral block 31) only under the correct upstream distribution; modifying earlier blocks induces distribution shift that degrades downstream approximations. (3) The reconstruction wall: approaches that factor weights into quantized components amplify errors through cross-terms, making direct quantization strictly superior. (4) Linearity increases with depth: Mistral 7B exhibits a progression from R^2 = 0.17 (block 0) to R^2 = 0.93 (block 31), indicating a division between nonlinear feature construction and linear refinement. (5) Approximately 30 percent of tokens are computationally easy, confirmed via exit heads and KL divergence sensitivity.
  We demonstrate that single-block linear replacement achieves 34x compression with a 1.71 perplexity increase on the final block of Mistral 7B, while multi-block replacement fails due to residual error accumulation and distribution shift. These findings suggest fundamental limits to static post-training compression and motivate adaptive, per-token computation as a more effective direction.

</details>


### [133] [MGDA-Decoupled: Geometry-Aware Multi-Objective Optimisation for DPO-based LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20685)
*Andor Vári-Kakas,Ji Won Park,Natasa Tagasovska*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MGDA-Decoupled是一种基于几何的多目标优化算法，用于平衡LLM对齐中的多个目标（如帮助性、真实性、无害性），在DPO框架内实现更公平的权衡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM对齐方法通常采用固定的目标加权方式，这会导致程序性不公平——难以优化的目标或少数目标被系统性地低估。需要一种能够更公平地权衡多个潜在冲突目标的方法

Method: 提出MGDA-Decoupled算法，这是一种基于几何的多目标优化方法，通过考虑每个目标的收敛动态来寻找共享下降方向。该方法完全在轻量级的直接偏好优化（DPO）范式内运行，不依赖强化学习或显式奖励模型

Result: 在UltraFeedback数据集上的实验表明，基于几何的方法（特别是MGDA-Decoupled）相对于黄金响应获得了最高的胜率，无论是整体还是针对每个具体目标

Conclusion: MGDA-Decoupled通过几何感知的多目标优化，在LLM对齐中实现了更公平的目标权衡，优于依赖固定加权或强化学习的方法，为平衡多个潜在冲突目标提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Aligning large language models (LLMs) to desirable human values requires balancing multiple, potentially conflicting objectives such as helpfulness, truthfulness, and harmlessness, which presents a multi-objective optimisation challenge. Most alignment pipelines rely on a fixed scalarisation of these objectives, which can introduce procedural unfairness by systematically under-weighting harder-to-optimise or minority objectives. To promote more equitable trade-offs, we introduce MGDA-Decoupled, a geometry-based multi-objective optimisation algorithm that finds a shared descent direction while explicitly accounting for each objective's convergence dynamics. In contrast to prior methods that depend on reinforcement learning (e.g., GAPO) or explicit reward models (e.g., MODPO), our approach operates entirely within the lightweight Direct Preference Optimisation (DPO) paradigm. Experiments on the UltraFeedback dataset show that geometry-aware methods -- and MGDA-Decoupled in particular -- achieve the highest win rates against golden responses, both overall and per objective.

</details>


### [134] [Generative Flow Networks for Model Adaptation in Digital Twins of Natural Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20707)
*Pascal Archambault,Houari Sahraoui,Eugene Syriani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于GFlowNet的数字孪生模型自适应方法，用于自然系统的数字孪生校准，通过生成建模处理观测稀疏和参数不确定性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然系统的数字孪生需要与物理系统保持对齐，但物理系统随时间演化、只能部分观测，且通常使用参数无法直接测量的机理模拟器建模。稀疏和间接的观测往往无法确定唯一最优的校准方案，导致多个模拟器参数化方案与现有证据兼容。

Method: 采用基于GFlowNet的方法，将模型自适应问题表述为完整模拟器配置的生成建模问题，使得合理的参数化方案能够以与模拟和观测行为一致性相关的奖励成比例的概率进行采样。

Result: 在基于机理番茄模型的受控环境农业案例研究中，学习到的策略能够恢复自适应景观的主要区域，检索出强校准假设，并在不确定性条件下保留多个合理的配置方案。

Conclusion: GFlowNet方法为数字孪生模型自适应提供了一种有效的生成建模框架，能够处理观测稀疏性和参数不确定性，为自然系统的数字孪生校准提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Digital twins of natural systems must remain aligned with physical systems that evolve over time, are only partially observed, and are typically modeled by mechanistic simulators whose parameters cannot be measured directly. In such settings, model adaptation is naturally posed as a simulation-based inference problem. However, sparse and indirect observations often fail to identify a unique and optimal calibration, leaving several simulator parameterizations compatible with the available evidence. This article presents a GFlowNet-based approach to model adaptation for digital twins of natural systems. We formulate adaptation as a generative modeling problem over complete simulator configurations, so that plausible parameterizations can be sampled with probability proportional to a reward derived from agreement between simulated and observed behavior. Using a controlled environment agriculture case study based on a mechanistic tomato model, we show that the learned policy recovers dominant regions of the adaptation landscape, retrieves strong calibration hypotheses, and preserves multiple plausible configurations under uncertainty.

</details>


### [135] [COMPASS: COntinual Multilingual PEFT with Adaptive Semantic Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20720)
*Noah Flynn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COMPASS是一个数据驱动的多语言LLM适配框架，通过分布感知采样选择辅助数据训练语言特定适配器，减少负向跨语言干扰，并在持续学习中动态更新适配器以适应数据分布变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多语言微调常因负向跨语言干扰导致性能下降，不同语言间存在性能差异。需要一种能最大化正向跨语言迁移、最小化干扰的高效多语言适配方法，并能在动态环境中持续适应数据分布变化。

Method: 1) 使用参数高效微调(PEFT)训练轻量级语言特定适配器；2) 核心是分布感知采样策略：利用多语言嵌入和聚类识别现有训练数据与目标使用分布之间的语义差距；3) 优先选择代表性不足语义簇的辅助数据；4) 扩展为持续学习框架COMPASS-ECDA，监控生产中的数据分布漂移并动态更新适配器，平衡新数据适应与现有知识保留。

Result: 在三种模型架构(Phi-4-Mini, Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen2.5-7B)和多个多语言基准(Global-MMLU, MMLU-ProX)上，包括未见的长上下文任务(OneRuler)，COMPASS始终优于基于语言相似性的基线方法。

Conclusion: COMPASS提供了一个有效、高效且可持续的解决方案，用于在动态环境中开发和维护高性能多语言模型，通过数据中心的适配策略解决了多语言微调中的负向干扰问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often exhibit performance disparities across languages, with naive multilingual fine-tuning frequently degrading performance due to negative cross-lingual interference. To address this, we introduce COMPASS (COntinual Multilingual PEFT with Adaptive Semantic Sampling), a novel data-centric framework for adapting LLMs to target languages. COMPASS leverages parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) by training lightweight, language-specific adapters on a judiciously selected subset of auxiliary multilingual data. The core of our method is a distribution-aware sampling strategy that uses multilingual embeddings and clustering to identify semantic gaps between existing training data and a target usage distribution. By prioritizing auxiliary data from under-represented semantic clusters, COMPASS maximizes positive cross-lingual transfer while minimizing interference. We extend this into a continual learning framework, COMPASS-ECDA, which monitors for data distribution shifts in production and dynamically updates adapters to prevent model staleness, balancing adaptation to new data with the preservation of existing knowledge. Across three different model architectures (Phi-4-Mini, Llama-3.1-8B, and Qwen2.5-7B) and multiple challenging multilingual benchmarks (Global-MMLU, MMLU-ProX), including unseen long-context tasks (OneRuler), we demonstrate that COMPASS consistently outperforms baseline methods guided by linguistic similarity, providing an effective, efficient, and sustainable solution for developing and maintaining high-performing multilingual models in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [136] [Supplement Generation Training for Enhancing Agentic Task Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20727)
*Young Min Cho,Daniele Bonadiman,Divya Bhargavi,Tamer Alkhouli,Salvatore Romeo,Dongwei Jiang,Khushbu Pahwa,Yubin Ge,Etsuko Ishii,Monica Sunkara,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Supplement Generation Training (SGT)方法，训练小型LLM生成补充文本，帮助大型LLM更有效地完成任务，避免对大型模型进行昂贵的重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练大型基础模型用于智能体任务成本高昂、迭代周期长且模型快速过时。传统方法需要为每个新任务或领域对大型模型进行后训练，这既不高效也不可持续。

Method: SGT训练一个较小的LLM来生成有用的补充文本，这些文本附加到原始输入中，帮助较大的LLM更有效地解决任务。这些轻量级模型可以动态适应任务需求，在不修改底层大型模型的情况下提升性能。

Result: 该方法将任务特定优化与大型基础模型解耦，实现了更灵活、成本效益更高的LLM驱动智能体在现实应用中的部署。

Conclusion: SGT提供了一种高效可持续的策略，通过训练小型补充生成模型来增强大型LLM的性能，避免了大规模模型重新训练的成本和复杂性。

Abstract: Training large foundation models for agentic tasks is increasingly impractical due to the high computational costs, long iteration cycles, and rapid obsolescence as new models are continuously released. Instead of post-training massive models for every new task or domain, we propose Supplement Generation Training (SGT), a more efficient and sustainable strategy. SGT trains a smaller LLM to generate useful supplemental text that, when appended to the original input, helps the larger LLM solve the task more effectively. These lightweight models can dynamically adapt supplements to task requirements, improving performance without modifying the underlying large models. This approach decouples task-specific optimization from large foundation models and enables more flexible, cost-effective deployment of LLM-powered agents in real-world applications.

</details>


### [137] [Near-Future Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20733)
*Chuanyu Qin,Chenxu Yang,Qingyi Si,Naibin Gu,Dingyu Yao,Zheng Lin,Peng Fu,Nan Duan,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NPO提出了一种混合策略优化方法，通过从同一训练运行中的未来检查点获取辅助轨迹，平衡轨迹质量与方差成本，提升RLVR性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合策略方法存在局限性：外部教师轨迹质量高但分布差异大，历史训练轨迹分布接近但质量受限。两者都无法同时满足"足够强"（高Q值，更多新知识）和"足够近"（低V值，更易吸收）的条件来最大化有效学习信号S=Q/V。

Method: 提出NPO（Near-Future Policy Optimization），从策略自身的近未来版本（同一训练运行中的后续检查点）获取辅助轨迹。该方法自然平衡轨迹质量与方差成本。进一步提出AutoNPO自适应变体，根据在线训练信号自动触发干预，并选择最大化S的指导检查点。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct with GRPO上，NPO将平均性能从57.88提升至62.84，AutoNPO进一步提升至63.15，在加速收敛的同时提高了最终性能上限。

Conclusion: NPO通过利用策略自身的近未来版本作为辅助轨迹源，有效解决了现有混合策略方法的局限性，在提升RLVR性能的同时加速了收敛过程。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a core post-training recipe. Introducing suitable off-policy trajectories into on-policy exploration accelerates RLVR convergence and raises the performance ceiling, yet finding a source of such trajectories remains the key challenge. Existing mixed-policy methods either import trajectories from external teachers (high-quality but distributionally far) or replay past training trajectories (close but capped in quality), and neither simultaneously satisfies the strong enough (higher $Q$ , more new knowledge to learn) and close enough (lower $V$ , more readily absorbed) conditions required to maximize the effective learning signal $\mathcal{S} = Q/V$. We propose \textbf{N}ear-Future \textbf{P}olicy \textbf{O}ptimization (\textbf{NPO}), a simple mixed-policy scheme that learns from a policy's own near-future self: a later checkpoint from the same training run is a natural source of auxiliary trajectories that is both stronger than the current policy and closer than any external source, directly balancing trajectory quality against variance cost. We validate NPO through two manual interventions, early-stage bootstrapping and late-stage plateau breakthrough, and further propose \textbf{AutoNPO},an adaptive variant that automatically triggers interventions from online training signals and selects the guide checkpoint that maximizes $S$. On Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct with GRPO, NPO improves average performance from 57.88 to 62.84, and AutoNPO pushes it to 63.15, raising the final performance ceiling while accelerating convergence.

</details>


### [138] [Relative Entropy Estimation in Function Space: Theory and Applications to Trajectory Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20775)
*Chao Wang,Luca Nepote,Giulio Franzese,Pietro Michiardi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种估计函数空间概率测度间KL散度的通用框架，用于评估轨迹推断方法，解决部分可观测性下的评估难题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹推断(TI)从快照数据恢复潜在动态过程，但破坏性测量使路径空间定律无法从有限边际中识别，现有评估协议(边际预测)有限且不一致

Method: 引入估计函数空间概率测度间KL散度的通用框架，开发可扩展至现实快照数据集的数据驱动估计器

Result: 在基准测试中，估计的函数KL与解析KL高度匹配；应用于合成和真实scRNA-seq数据集，显示当前评估指标常给出不一致评估，而路径空间KL能实现轨迹推断方法的连贯比较并揭示推断动态中的差异

Conclusion: 函数KL可作为评估部分可观测性下轨迹推断的原则性标准，提供比现有边际预测更一致的评估框架

Abstract: Trajectory Inference (TI) seeks to recover latent dynamical processes from snapshot data, where only independent samples from time-indexed marginals are observed. In applications such as single-cell genomics, destructive measurements make path-space laws non-identifiable from finitely many marginals, leaving held-out marginal prediction as the dominant but limited evaluation protocol. We introduce a general framework for estimating the Kullback-Leibler divergence (KL) divergence between probability measures on function space, yielding a tractable, data-driven estimator that is scalable to realistic snapshot datasets. We validate the accuracy of our estimator on a benchmark suite, where the estimated functional KL closely matches the analytic KL. Applying this framework to synthetic and real scRNA-seq datasets, we show that current evaluation metrics often give inconsistent assessments, whereas path-space KL enables a coherent comparison of trajectory inference methods and exposes discrepancies in inferred dynamics, especially in regions with sparse or missing data. These results support functional KL as a principled criterion for evaluating trajectory inference under partial observability.

</details>


### [139] [Efficient Multi-Cohort Inference for Long-Term Effects and Lifetime Value in A/B Testing with User Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20777)
*Dario Simionato,Andrea Tonon,Mingxue Wang,Weiguo Wang,Tong Gui,Xiaoyue Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种在短期多队列A/B测试中估计长期处理效应和剩余用户价值的方法，解决传统短期指标无法捕捉用户流失影响的问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流媒体平台用户流失成本极高，但传统A/B测试仅使用有限实验周期内的结果进行评估。即使考虑短期和预测的长期参与度指标，仍可能无法捕捉处理对用户留存的影响，导致干预措施在短期看似有益、长期看似中性，但因用户流失而产生更低总价值

Method: 引入逆方差加权估计器，结合多个队列估计值来高效估计时变处理效应，减少方差。将估计的处理轨迹建模为参数衰减，以恢复渐近处理效应和随时间累积的价值。框架支持在单个实验中同时评估稳态影响和剩余用户价值

Result: 实证结果显示，在估计长期处理效应和剩余用户价值变化方面提高了精度，并识别出仅依赖短期或长期指标会导致错误产品决策的场景

Conclusion: 该方法解决了传统A/B测试在评估用户留存影响方面的局限性，为流媒体平台提供了更全面的干预效果评估框架，能够更准确地捕捉处理对用户长期价值和流失的影响

Abstract: In streaming platforms churn is extremely costly, yet A/B tests are typically evaluated using outcomes observed within a limited experimental horizon. Even when both short- and predicted long-term engagement metrics are considered, they may fail to capture how a treatment affects users' retention. Consequently, an intervention may appear beneficial in the short term and neutral in the long term while still generating lower total value than the control due to users churn.
  To address this limitation, we introduce a method that estimates long-term treatment effects (LTE) and residual lifetime value change ($ΔERLV$) in short multi-cohort A/B tests under user learning. To estimate time-varying treatment effects efficiently, we introduce an inverse-variance weighted estimator that combines multiple cohorts estimates, reducing variance relative to standard approaches in the literature. The estimated treatment trajectory is then modeled as a parametric decay to recover both the asymptotic treatment effect and the cumulative value generated over time.
  Our framework enables simultaneous evaluation of steady-state impact and residual user value within a single experiment. Empirical results show improved precision in estimating LTE and $ΔERLV$ and identify scenarios in which relying on either short-term or long-term metrics alone would lead to incorrect product decisions.

</details>


### [140] [Stream-CQSA: Avoiding Out-of-Memory in Attention Computation via Flexible Workload Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20819)
*Yiming Bian,Joshua M. Akey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Stream-CQSA：基于循环仲裁集理论分解注意力机制，实现内存自适应调度，可在单GPU上执行十亿token序列的精确注意力计算


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文大语言模型的可扩展性受限于精确自注意力的二次方内存成本，导致现代硬件上出现内存不足故障。现有方法虽然将内存效率提升到近线性复杂度，但假设完整的查询、键和值张量都能放入设备内存，这一假设限制了实际应用。

Method: 引入CQS Divide操作，基于循环仲裁集理论将注意力分解为一组独立的子序列计算，其重组结果与完整序列注意力完全相同。在此基础上提出Stream-CQSA内存自适应调度框架，将注意力划分为适合任意内存预算的子问题，将注意力从逻辑上的单一操作转变为可调度任务集合。

Result: 实验证明该方法具有可预测的内存扩展性，通过流式处理可在单GPU上执行十亿token序列的精确注意力计算，无需改变注意力的数学定义或引入近似误差。

Conclusion: Stream-CQSA通过分解和调度策略突破了长序列注意力计算的内存限制，实现了精确注意力在资源受限环境下的可扩展执行，为超长上下文模型的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The scalability of long-context large language models is fundamentally limited by the quadratic memory cost of exact self-attention, which often leads to out-of-memory (OOM) failures on modern hardware. Existing methods improve memory efficiency to near-linear complexity, while assuming that the full query, key, and value tensors fit in device memory. In this work, we remove this assumption by introducing CQS Divide, an operation derived from cyclic quorum sets (CQS) theory that decomposes attention into a set of independent subsequence computations whose recomposition yields exactly the same result as full-sequence attention. Exploiting this decomposition, we introduce Stream-CQSA, a memory-adaptive scheduling framework that partitions attention into subproblems that fit within arbitrary memory budgets. This recasts attention from a logically monolithic operation into a collection of schedulable tasks, enabling flexible execution across devices without inter-device communication. Experiments demonstrate predictable memory scaling and show that exact attention over billion-token sequences can be executed on a single GPU via streaming, without changing the underlying mathematical definition of attention or introducing approximation error.

</details>


### [141] [Closing the Domain Gap in Biomedical Imaging by In-Context Control Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20824)
*Ana Sanchez-Fernandez,Thomas Pinetz,Werner Zellinger,Günter Klambauer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CS-ARM-BN是一种基于元学习的适应方法，利用阴性对照样本解决生物医学成像中的批次效应问题，在JUMP-CP数据集上显著提升了模型在新实验批次上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学成像中的批次效应是系统性技术变异，与生物信号无关，严重破坏实验可重复性，导致深度学习系统在新实验批次上性能下降，阻碍其实际应用。尽管多年研究，现有方法未能有效解决深度学习模型的这一性能差距。

Method: 提出Control-Stabilized Adaptive Risk Minimization via Batch Normalization (CS-ARM-BN)，一种元学习适应方法，利用阴性对照样本。这些未受扰动的参考图像在每个实验批次中都有设计存在，可作为稳定的适应上下文。该方法特别适用于存在强域偏移的情况，如不同实验室生成的数据。

Result: 在JUMP-CP数据集上的机制作用分类任务中，标准ResNet在训练域上的准确率为0.939±0.005，在新实验批次数据上降至0.862±0.060。基础模型即使经过典型变异归一化也无法弥合这一差距。CS-ARM-BN首次通过元学习方法弥合了域差距，达到0.935±0.018的准确率。当新批次存在强域偏移时，利用对照样本可稳定元学习方法。

Conclusion: 生物医学成像数据中的批次效应可以通过原则性的上下文适应方法有效中和，这使得深度学习模型在实际应用中变得可用且高效。利用阴性对照样本的元学习适应是解决批次效应问题的有效途径。

Abstract: The central problem in biomedical imaging are batch effects: systematic technical variations unrelated to the biological signal of interest. These batch effects critically undermine experimental reproducibility and are the primary cause of failure of deep learning systems on new experimental batches, preventing their practical use in the real world. Despite years of research, no method has succeeded in closing this performance gap for deep learning models. We propose Control-Stabilized Adaptive Risk Minimization via Batch Normalization (CS-ARM-BN), a meta-learning adaptation method that exploits negative control samples. Such unperturbed reference images are present in every experimental batch by design and serve as stable context for adaptation. We validate our novel method on Mechanism-of-Action (MoA) classification, a crucial task for drug discovery, on the large-scale JUMP-CP dataset. The accuracy of standard ResNets drops from 0.939 $\pm$ 0.005, on the training domain, to 0.862 $\pm$ 0.060 on data from new experimental batches. Foundation models, even after Typical Variation Normalization, fail to close this gap. We are the first to show that meta-learning approaches close the domain gap by achieving 0.935 $\pm$ 0.018. If the new experimental batches exhibit strong domain shifts, such as being generated in a different lab, meta-learning approaches can be stabilized with control samples, which are always available in biomedical experiments. Our work shows that batch effects in bioimaging data can be effectively neutralized through principled in-context adaptation, which also makes them practically usable and efficient.

</details>


### [142] [FedSIR: Spectral Client Identification and Relabeling for Federated Learning with Noisy Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20825)
*Sina Gholami,Abdulmoneam Ali,Tania Haghighi,Ahmed Arafa,Minhaj Nur Alam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedSIR是一个用于噪声标签联邦学习的多阶段框架，通过分析客户端特征表示的谱结构来识别和缓解标签噪声，包含干净客户端识别、谱参考重标注和噪声感知训练三个关键组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习允许在不共享原始数据的情况下进行协作模型训练，但分布式客户端中存在的噪声标签会严重降低学习性能。现有方法主要依赖设计噪声容忍损失函数或利用训练过程中的损失动态，缺乏对特征表示谱结构的利用。

Method: FedSIR包含三个关键组件：1) 通过分析类特定特征子空间的谱一致性来识别干净和噪声客户端，通信开销最小；2) 干净客户端提供谱参考，使噪声客户端能够使用主导类方向和残差子空间重新标注可能损坏的样本；3) 采用噪声感知训练策略，集成对数调整损失、知识蒸馏和距离感知聚合，进一步稳定联邦优化。

Result: 在标准联邦学习基准测试上的广泛实验表明，FedSIR在噪声标签联邦学习任务中持续优于最先进的方法。

Conclusion: FedSIR通过利用客户端特征表示的谱结构来识别和缓解标签噪声，为噪声标签联邦学习提供了一个有效的多阶段框架，在通信开销最小的情况下实现了优越的性能。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training without sharing raw data; however, the presence of noisy labels across distributed clients can severely degrade the learning performance. In this paper, we propose FedSIR, a multi-stage framework for robust FL under noisy labels. Different from existing approaches that mainly rely on designing noise-tolerant loss functions or exploiting loss dynamics during training, our method leverages the spectral structure of client feature representations to identify and mitigate label noise.
  Our framework consists of three key components. First, we identify clean and noisy clients by analyzing the spectral consistency of class-wise feature subspaces with minimal communication overhead. Second, clean clients provide spectral references that enable noisy clients to relabel potentially corrupted samples using both dominant class directions and residual subspaces. Third, we employ a noise-aware training strategy that integrates logit-adjusted loss, knowledge distillation, and distance-aware aggregation to further stabilize federated optimization. Extensive experiments on standard FL benchmarks demonstrate that FedSIR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods for FL with noisy labels. The code is available at https://github.com/sinagh72/FedSIR.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [Debiasing optimal transport: classical and entropic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19981)
*Pierre-Cyril Aubin-Frankowski,Virginie Ehrlacher,Gabriele Todeschi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究最优传输中成本函数的去偏性概念，重点关注概率测度空间上不同正则化参数ε下的熵正则化最优传输


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究成本函数的去偏性概念，即对称成本函数满足c(x,y) ≥ ½c(x,x) + ½c(y,y)的性质。这种性质可以视为平方测地距离中点恒等式的推广，对于理解最优传输中不同正则化参数下的成本函数性质具有重要意义

Method: 通过等价特征表示c(x,y)=inf_{z∈Z}ψ(x,z)+ψ(y,z)来研究去偏性。重点关注概率测度空间上熵正则化最优传输的三个参数区间：经典最优传输(ε=0)、熵最优传输(ε>0)和最大均值差异(ε=+∞)。对于ε∈(0,+∞]，使用负定地面成本或诱导核的连续性和正定性等充分条件，通过凸-非凹极小极大论证处理

Result: 将去偏性分析扩展到不平衡最优传输设置，推广了Feydy等人和Sejourne等人的研究结果。作为副产品，推导出熵最优传输的新分解公式

Conclusion: 系统地研究了最优传输成本函数的去偏性概念，建立了不同正则化参数下熵正则化最优传输的去偏性条件，并将结果推广到不平衡最优传输设置，为理解最优传输中不同正则化机制下的成本函数性质提供了理论框架

Abstract: We study the notion of debiasability for cost functions arising in optimal transport. We call a symmetric cost function $c:\mathscr{X}\times\mathscr{X}\to\mathbb{R}\cup\{+\infty\}$ debiasable if it satisfies $c(x,y)\ge \tfrac{1}{2}c(x,x)+\tfrac{1}{2}c(y,y)$ for all $x,y\in\mathscr{X}$. Building on an equivalent characterization by an inf-representation $c(x,y)=\inf_{z\in\mathscr{Z}}ψ(x,z)+ψ(y,z)$ for some set $\mathscr{Z}$ and some function $ψ: \mathscr{X}\times \mathscr{Z} \to \mathbb{R} \cup \{+\infty\}$, interpreted as a generalization of the midpoint identity for squared geodesic distances, we investigate the debiasability of costs defined on spaces of probability measures. Our primary focus is the entropic regularization of optimal transport across different regimes of the regularization parameter $\varepsilon \in [0,+\infty]$, encompassing classical optimal transport ($\varepsilon=0$), entropic optimal transport ($\varepsilon>0$), and the Maximum Mean Discrepancy ($\varepsilon=+\infty$). For $\varepsilon \in (0,+\infty]$, we investigate sufficient conditions, such as negative definiteness of the ground cost or continuity and positive definiteness of the induced kernel, handled then via a convex-nonconcave minimax argument. All our results extend naturally to unbalanced optimal transport settings and we generalize in this way the findings of \cite{feydy2019interpolating} and \cite{sejourne2019sinkhorn}. As a byproduct, we derive novel decomposition formulas for entropic optimal transport, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [144] [Covariance Steering of Discrete-Time Markov Jump Linear Systems with Multiplicative Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19994)
*Fangji Wang,Siddhartha Ganguly,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了具有状态和控制依赖乘性噪声的离散时间马尔可夫跳变线性系统的有限时域协方差控制问题，提出了基于提升状态表述的SDP重构方法，并研究了机会约束下的协方差控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有状态和控制依赖乘性噪声的马尔可夫跳变线性系统的协方差控制问题，这类系统在金融等应用中常见，但现有方法通常假设纯仿射状态反馈足够，而实际上需要更复杂的控制结构。

Method: 1) 证明可行控制可表示为模态依赖的线性反馈加前馈和独立随机分量；2) 引入提升状态表述，将均值和协方差信息嵌入统一的二阶矩描述；3) 建立与原问题的等价性，获得无损失松弛和SDP重构；4) 对机会约束问题推导可处理的凸替代并建立迭代参考更新方案。

Result: 1) 证明了纯仿射状态反馈在乘性噪声下不足，需要更复杂的控制结构；2) 建立了提升状态表述与原问题的等价性；3) 获得了无约束情况下的SDP重构；4) 为机会约束问题提供了可处理的凸替代和减少保守性的迭代方案；5) 在金融应用上进行了数值验证。

Conclusion: 该论文为具有乘性噪声的马尔可夫跳变线性系统协方差控制问题提供了系统性的解决方案，通过提升状态表述实现了精确的SDP重构，并为机会约束问题提供了有效的凸近似方法，在金融等应用中具有实用价值。

Abstract: We study a finite-horizon covariance steering problem for discrete-time Markov jump linear systems (MJLS) with both state- and control-dependent multiplicative noise. The objective is to minimize a quadratic running cost while steering the system from given mode-conditioned initial means and covariances to a prescribed terminal mean and covariance. We first show that, without loss of generality, feasible controls may be represented by mode-dependent linear feedback together with feedforward and independent random components, and we highlight that, in contrast to the case without multiplicative noise, a purely affine state-feedback law does not in general suffice. To this end, we introduce a lifted-state formulation that embeds the mean and covariance information into a unified second-moment description, and we prove that the resulting lifted problem is equivalent to the original covariance steering problem formulation. This leads to a lossless relaxation in moment variables and an SDP reformulation for the unconstrained case. We further study chance-constrained covariance steering with ball and half-space constraints on the state and control, derive tractable sufficient convex surrogates, and establish an iterative reference-update scheme to reduce conservatism. Numerical experiments on a finance application illustrate our results.

</details>


### [145] [Forward-looking evolutionary game dynamics subject to exploration cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20029)
*Hidekazu Yoshioka*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 将经典进化博弈动力学扩展为包含前瞻性行为和探索成本的前瞻性进化博弈动力学框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统进化博弈动力学仅基于智能体的瞬时行动选择，缺乏对前瞻性行为和探索成本的考虑。本文旨在扩展经典框架，纳入这两个重要元素，以更准确地描述智能体的决策过程。

Method: 基于成对比较协议（涵盖复制动态和logit模型等主要进化博弈动力学），构建数学框架：智能体通过支付成本来更新行动以最大化效用或其相对差异。将前瞻性行为建模为进化博弈动力学与静态Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程（平均场博弈）的耦合。探索成本及其约束自然地作为最优拉格朗日乘子函数纳入方程中。

Result: 证明在特定条件下，所提出的进化博弈动力学存在唯一解。通过计算实验研究了一维和二维问题，验证了理论框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 成功构建了包含前瞻性行为和探索成本的前瞻性进化博弈动力学框架，该框架将经典进化博弈动力学与平均场博弈理论相结合，为研究智能体在考虑未来收益和探索成本时的决策行为提供了新的数学工具。

Abstract: We extend classical evolutionary game dynamics based on the momentary action choices of agents by accounting for two elements: forward-looking behavior and exploration cost. We focus on pairwise comparison protocols that cover major evolutionary game dynamics, such as replicator and logit models. In the proposed mathematical framework, agents update their actions by paying a cost so that a utility or its relative difference is maximized. We show that forward-looking behavior can be modeled as a coupling between the evolutionary game dynamic and static Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation: a mean field game. The exploration cost and its constraint are naturally related to these equations as a function of the optimal Lagrangian multiplier serving as a relaxation parameter, and it is incorporated into the game as a constraint. We show that under certain conditions, our evolutionary game dynamic admits a unique solution. Finally, we computationally investigate one- and two-dimensional problems.

</details>


### [146] [Decision-Focused Federated Learning Under Heterogeneous Objectives and Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20031)
*Konstantinos Ziliaskopoulos,Alexander Vinel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文提出决策导向联邦学习框架，研究在预测-优化场景下，各客户端目标函数和约束条件不同时，如何通过SPO+损失函数分析异构性边界，并建立本地vs联邦决策规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究联邦学习在决策导向场景下的应用，即多个智能体使用预测模型作为下游线性优化问题的输入，且不允许原始数据交换。关键挑战在于客户端在目标函数和可行性约束方面都存在差异，需要分析这种异构性对决策质量的影响。

Method: 基于SPO+方法，使用可行域的支持函数表示，分离目标偏移（通过成本向量范数距离）和可行集偏移（通过约束集形状距离）。对于强凸可行域，利用优化器稳定性推导更紧的边界。建立本地vs联邦超额风险决策规则，通过SPO+风险判断联邦化何时能改善决策质量。

Result: 在强凸问题设置中，联邦学习表现出广泛鲁棒性；在多面体问题设置中，性能主要随约束异构性下降，特别是对于样本较多的客户端。实验表明，即使下游优化问题明显不同，直接实现FedAvg和SPO+的方法在强凸情况下仍能获得良好性能。

Conclusion: 决策导向联邦学习框架在客户端异构性存在时仍可行，特别是对于强凸可行域问题。异构性惩罚必须小于数据池化的统计优势时，联邦化才能改善决策质量。该方法为预测-优化场景下的联邦学习提供了理论分析和实用指导。

Abstract: We consider what we refer to as {Decision-Focused Federated Learning (DFFL)} framework, i.e., a predict-then-optimize approach employed by a collection of agents, where each agent's predictive model is an input to a downstream linear optimization problem, and no direct exchange of raw data is allowed. Importantly, clients can differ both in objective functions and in feasibility constraints. We build on the well-known SPO+ approach and develop heterogeneity bounds for the SPO+ surrogate loss in this case. This is accomplished by employing a support function representation of the feasible region, separating (i) objective shift via norm distances between the cost vectors and (ii) feasible-set shift via shape distances between the constraint sets. In the case of strongly convex feasible regions, sharper bounds are derived due to the optimizer stability. Building on these results, we define a heuristic local-versus-federated excess risk decision rule which, under SPO+ risk, gives a condition for when federation can be expected to improve decision quality: the heterogeneity penalty must be smaller than the statistical advantage of pooling data. We implement a FedAvg-style DFFL set of experiments on both polyhedral and strongly convex problems and show that federation is broadly robust in the strongly convex setting, while performance in the polyhedral setting degrades primarily with constraint heterogeneity, especially for clients with many samples. In other words, especially for the strongly convex case, an approach following a direct implementation of FedAvg and SPO+ can still yield promising performance even when the downstream optimization problems are noticeably different.

</details>


### [147] [A Benchmark of 25 Nonlinear Functions with Domain-Induced Discontinuity for Global Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20107)
*Peicong Cheng,Makoto Yamashita*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了包含25个具有域诱导不连续性的非线性优化问题的基准测试集（CPC基准），用于评估全局优化算法在可行域稀缺和结构不连续景观下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化基准测试集通常假设目标函数在整个搜索空间连续可定义，而实际工程问题常包含不可行区域和未定义评估。需要创建能反映这些现实挑战的测试基准，以推动全局优化算法在可行性稀缺和结构不连续场景下的研究。

Method: 设计了25个非线性优化问题，这些函数在其自然定义域内连续，但通过隐式嵌入不可行区域和未定义评估来创建域诱导不连续性。使用来自不同方法范式的六种代表性算法进行评估，分析基准的结构复杂性和区分能力。

Result: 数值结果显示，许多函数具有极小的可行区域，且在可行边界附近表现出强烈的精度敏感性，这使初始化、可行域发现和可靠目标评估变得复杂。基准测试对不同算法范式展现出清晰的区分能力。

Conclusion: CPC基准为全局优化研究提供了严格的、面向软件的测试平台，能够有效评估算法在可行性稀缺和结构不连续景观下的性能，具有明确的区分能力，有助于推动该领域的研究进展。

Abstract: A benchmark of 25 nonlinear optimization problems with domain-induced discontinuity is proposed to support the performance evaluation of global optimization algorithms under feasibility-scarce and structurally discontinuous landscapes. Referred to as the CPC Benchmark (Challenging Problems for Computation), the test suiteconsists of functions that are continuous on their natural domains, while infeasible regions and undefined evaluations are implicitly embedded in the objective, creating substantial challenges for global minimization. Six representative algorithms from diverse methodological paradigms are assessed to examine the structural complexity and discriminative capability of the benchmark. Numerical results show that many functions possess extremely small feasible regions and strong precision sensitivity near feasibility boundaries, complicating initialization, feasibility discovery, and reliable objective assessment. The findings demonstrate that the CPC benchmark provides clear discriminative power across algorithmic paradigms and offers a rigorous, software-oriented testbed for advancing research in global optimization.

</details>


### [148] [Robust Out-of-Distribution Stochastic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20147)
*Xianyu Li,Huan Xu,Xiaolin Huang,Chao Shang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的数据驱动框架——鲁棒分布外随机优化，用于在未见分布下进行决策，通过从相关数据分布中学习RKHS中的不确定性集，并融入min-max随机规划来获得鲁棒决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据驱动决策假设可以从目标分布收集历史数据，但实际中在决策前可能无法获得目标分布的任何数据。需要一种能够有效利用相关数据分布进行鲁棒决策的框架，以应对未见分布的情况。

Method: 提出鲁棒分布外随机优化框架：1）假设所有数据分布都是从分布上的元分布随机生成的；2）在再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）中从相关数据分布学习数据驱动的不确定性集，可调整保守程度；3）将该集融入min-max随机规划获得鲁棒决策；4）提出近似参数化和行生成策略以简化RKHS中的问题求解。

Result: 建立了分布生成随机性下不确定性集和解的严格分布外泛化保证。近似参数化具有可证明的有界次优性。在多物品报童问题和投资组合优化的广泛数值实验中，即使只有少量或中等数量的相关源可用，该框架在未见数据分布下也表现出优越的分布外性能。

Conclusion: 提出的鲁棒分布外随机优化框架能够有效利用相关数据分布进行鲁棒决策，在未见分布下表现出优越性能，为解决无法获得目标分布数据时的决策问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Data-driven decision-making under uncertainty typically presumes the collection of historical data from an unknown target probability distribution. However, one may have no access to any data from the target distribution prior to decision-making. To address this challenge, we propose robust out-of-distribution stochastic optimization, a novel data-driven framework that effectively utilizes relevant data distributions for robust decision-making under unseen distributions. A key feature of our framework is that all data distributions are assumed to be randomly generated from a meta-distribution over distributions. To describe uncertainty in distribution generation, we propose to learn a data-driven uncertainty set in a reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) from relevant data distributions, with adjustable conservatism. We then incorporate this set into a min-max stochastic program to derive robust decisions. Notably, under randomness of distribution generation, we establish rigorous out-of-distribution generalization guarantees for the uncertainty set as well as the solution. To ease problem-solving in RKHS, an approximate parametrization with a provably bounded suboptimality and a row generation strategy are presented. Extensive numerical experiments on multi-item newsvendor and portfolio optimization demonstrate the superior out-of-distribution performance of our decision-making framework under unseen data distribution, even when only a small or moderate number of relevant sources are available.

</details>


### [149] [On Reward-Balancing Methods for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20433)
*Simone Baroncini,Bahman Gharesifard,Giuseppe Notarstefano*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文研究奖励平衡方法，这是一种通过迭代调整奖励函数将强化学习问题转化为等价问题的新算法类别，使最优策略变得贪婪，并在最优控制框架下扩展该方法以处理模型不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究奖励平衡方法作为解决折扣回报强化学习问题的新算法类别，旨在通过变换奖励函数使最优策略变得贪婪，从而简化问题求解。

Method: 1. 对奖励平衡方法中的归一化过程进行理论分析，强调其代数结构；2. 引入控制理论重构，将奖励平衡过程重新表述为最优控制框架；3. 通过随机模型采样扩展方法以处理模型不确定性，提供归一化保证和随机波动概率界限；4. 在场景模型预测控制设置中使用最优控制框架。

Result: 通过仿真研究表明，在场景模型预测控制设置中使用提出的最优控制框架，相比当前最先进方法实现了性能改进。

Conclusion: 奖励平衡方法为强化学习问题提供了新的解决方案框架，通过将问题转化为最优控制问题并处理模型不确定性，在性能上超越了现有方法。

Abstract: This paper investigates the so-called reward-balancing methods, a novel class of algorithms for solving discounted-return reinforcement learning (RL) problems. These methods consist of iteratively adjusting the reward function to transform the RL problem into an equivalent one in which the optimal policies are greedy. For this procedure, referred to as normalization process, we provide a theoretical analysis of the involved transformations, emphasizing their algebraic structure. Then, we introduce a control-theoretic reformulation, recasting the reward-balancing procedure into an optimal control framework. The approach is further extended to address model uncertainty through stochastic model sampling, yielding normalization guarantees and probabilistic bounds on stochastic fluctuations. Using the proposed optimal control framework within a scenario model predictive control (MPC) setting, we demonstrate, through simulation studies, performance improvements over the current state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [150] [A unified framework for inexact adaptive stepsizes in the gradient methods, the conjugate gradient methods and the quasi-Newton methods for strictly convex quadratic optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20506)
*Zexian Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个用于严格凸二次优化的梯度法、共轭梯度法和拟牛顿法中非精确自适应步长的统一框架——近似最优步长，并建立了其全局收敛性和收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前对于共轭梯度法和拟牛顿法的非精确自适应步长研究非常稀少。虽然精确步长在这些方法中已有统一框架，但非精确自适应步长的统一框架仍然未知，这促使研究者提出一个统一的非精确自适应步长框架。

Method: 提出了一个称为"近似最优步长"的统一框架，适用于梯度法、共轭梯度法和拟牛顿法在严格凸二次优化问题中。通过探索近似最优步长与著名的Barzilai-Borwein（BB）步长之间的关系，建立了梯度法使用该步长的全局收敛性和收敛速率。

Result: 数值结果证实了梯度法、共轭梯度法和拟牛顿法使用该统一非精确自适应步长框架具有显著的数值优势。同时提出了一些关于这些方法使用近似最优步长的开放性问题。

Conclusion: 成功构建了一个适用于多种优化方法的非精确自适应步长统一框架，填补了该领域的研究空白，并为严格凸二次优化问题提供了有效的数值解决方案。

Abstract: The inexact adaptive stepsizes for the conjugate gradient method and the quasi-Newton method are very rare. The exact stepsizes in the gradient method, the conjugate gradient method and the quasi-Newton method for strictly convex quadratic optimization have a unified framework, while the unified framework for inexact adaptive stepsizes in the gradient method, the conjugate gradient method and the quasi-Newton method for strictly convex quadratic optimization still remains unknown. Based on the above observations, we propose a unified framework for inexact adaptive stepsizes in the gradient method, the conjugate gradient method and the quasi-Newton method for strictly convex quadratic optimization, which is called approximately optimal stepsize. The global convergence and the convergence rate of the gradient method with the approximately optimal stepsize are established by exploring the relation between the approximately optimal stepsize and the famous Barzilai-Borwein (BB) stepsizes. Some numerical results are presented, which confirm the remarkable numerical advantage of the gradient method, the conjugate gradient method and the quasi-Newton method with the unified framework for inexact adaptive stepsizes. Some open problems about the gradient method, the conjugate gradient method and the quasi-Newton method with approximately optimal stepsize are raised.

</details>


### [151] [Importance Sampling in Expensive Finite-Sum Optimization via Contextual Bandit Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20657)
*Matt Menickelly*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文重新审视了随机平均模型(SAM)方法，提出将子集选择的随机化问题建模为上下文多臂赌博机问题，并应用Exp4算法来生成采样分布，特别是在存在辅助信息(如低精度模拟、预训练模拟器或专家知识)的情况下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在计算科学工作流中，优化目标函数通常涉及多个模拟输出，且这些模拟可以(至少部分)并行执行。现有随机平均模型(SAM)方法需要在模型优化方法的每次迭代中仅对随机子集的模拟输出进行局部建模，但如何最佳地执行这种子集选择的随机化，特别是在存在有用辅助信息的情况下，是一个重要问题。

Method: 将SAM方法中的采样分布生成问题建模为上下文多臂赌博机问题，应用指数权重探索与利用专家算法(Exp4)。该方法利用辅助信息(如替代低精度模拟、预训练模拟器或人类/AI模型的领域专业知识)来指导子集选择。

Result: 在合成问题上提供了初步数值结果，表明所提出的方法能够有效利用辅助信息来改进随机子集选择策略。

Conclusion: 通过将SAM方法的子集选择问题形式化为上下文赌博机问题并应用Exp4算法，能够更智能地利用辅助信息来指导随机化过程，从而提高计算科学工作流中并行模拟优化的效率。

Abstract: In computational science workflows, it is often the case that 1) objective functions for optimization involve multiple simulation outputs, and 2) those simulations can be performed (at least partially) in parallel. In this work, we reexamine past work on a class of randomized algorithms, stochastic average model (SAM) methods. SAM methods are conceptually similar to stochastic average gradient methods, and effectively require that only randomized subsets of simulation outputs be locally modeled in each iteration of a model-based optimization method. This work focuses on the question of how best to perform this randomization of subset selection, especially in settings where there exists useful side information such as alternative lower-fidelity simulations, pre-trained emulators or domain expertise from humans or AI models. In particular, we consider the problem of generating sampling distributions for SAM methods as a contextual bandit problem and we apply the Exponential weights algorithm for Exploration and Exploitation with Experts (Exp4). We provide some preliminary numerical results on synthetic problems.

</details>


### [152] [Solving Minimax Problems with Bilinear Objectives with ADMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20832)
*Bob Wilson*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对双线性极小极大问题的ADMM算法：当目标函数为双线性时，ADMM中的邻近算子可精确简化为置信区域S上的广义投影，避免了近似或线性化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在双线性极小极大问题中应用ADMM时面临的挑战：通常需要计算邻近算子，而该算子与原问题难度相当。本文旨在寻找一种精确简化方法，使ADMM能高效应用于此类问题。

Method: 当目标函数g(c;β)=c^T A β为双线性时，证明ADMM中的邻近算子可精确简化为置信区域S上的广义投影。由此得到的ADMM算法交替执行：(1) 在S上的广义投影；(2) 在C上的欧几里得投影。

Result: 成功推导出双线性极小极大问题的精确简化：邻近算子简化为广义投影，避免了近似处理。算法结构清晰，收敛性可讨论。

Conclusion: 对于双线性极小极大问题，ADMM算法中的邻近算子可精确简化为广义投影，这为高效求解此类问题提供了理论基础和算法框架。

Abstract: We consider minimax (saddle-point) problems of the form max_{c \in C} min_{β\in S} g(c; β), where C and S are compact convex sets, and g is concave-convex. Applying the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) requires evaluating a proximal operator that is, in general, as hard as the original problem. We show that when the outcome function g is bilinear, i.e. g(c; β) = c^T A β, the proximal operator reduces to a generalized projection onto the confidence region S. This reduction is exact -- it involves no approximation or linearization. The resulting ADMM algorithm alternates between (i) a generalized projection onto S and (ii) a Euclidean projection onto C. We describe the derivation, state the algorithm, and discuss convergence.

</details>
